# Kennel Onboarding — Run Log

Append one entry per daily run (newest at top). Keep it terse.

Format:
```
## YYYY-MM-DD — <shortName> (<region>)
- Source: <TYPE> — <url/id>
- Outcome: <PR #123 | branch onboard/... ready to push | blocked: reason>
- Events verified: <count>, date range <…>
- Historical backfill: <count or none>
- Follow-ups: <anything deferred>
```

> **CONFIG BATCH C (2026-08-12) — 3 new kennels + 1 source-add, part of the 20-kennel backlog rescue.**
> Built from four handoffs generated 2026-07-21 → 08-09 that sat uncommitted for weeks (see
> `handoffs/retros/2026-08-11-backlog-rescue-retro.md`): **Devon Lunatics H3 (`dlh3-gb`, Exeter,
> GOOGLE_CALENDAR) · Isca H3 (`isca-h3`, Exeter, GOOGLE_CALENDAR) · JaxH3 (`jax-h3`, Jacksonville FL,
> ICAL_FEED source-add on an existing sourceless kennel) · Mickleover H3 (`mickleover-h3`, East
> Midlands, GOOGLE_SHEETS).** Config-only across all four adapters, plus one small opt-in
> `GoogleSheetsConfig.requireRunNumber` flag (~5 lines + 2 tests) to cleanly drop Mickleover's
> unnumbered winter "social" rows. New Exeter METRO (shared by the two Devon kennels) + East Midlands
> METRO (carried here since the sibling Quorn H3 handoff hasn't shipped). Re-verified all 4 sources
> fresh at build time rather than trusting the stale handoffs — every archive had grown since
> research (Isca 359→401 events, Mickleover #362→#364). 4 self-hosted logos, all magic-byte verified.
> Full retro: `handoffs/retros/2026-08-09-config-batch-retro.md`.
>
> **🔴 STATUS (2026-08-11) — BACKLOG RESCUE: 20 handoffs recovered, 0 onboarded yet.** The daily runs
> of **2026-07-16 → 2026-08-11** produced 20 complete handoffs that were **never committed** — they
> existed only as untracked files in the main repo working tree, so `origin/main` never moved and
> every other checkout saw a folder that looked complete. This is the **third recurrence** of one root
> cause (4 kennels 2026-07-05 → 6 kennels 2026-07-15 → **20 now**), and the fix both prior retros
> prescribed had never been built. All 20 handoffs + 848 lines of run-log / platform-note / queue
> edits are now in git. Retro: `handoffs/retros/2026-08-11-backlog-rescue-retro.md`.
>
> Two silent detection bugs were found and fixed: `copy-newest-handoff.sh` judged "implemented" by
> `onboard/<code>-*` branch existence (a never-committed handoff has no branch → reported as **done**),
> and the audit snippet in `handoffs/README.md` was **broken under zsh** (no word-splitting + grep
> treats an embedded newline as pattern-OR → every handoff naming >1 kennelCode false-passed; that is
> how `donnington-h3` hid). The helper now tests **seed presence**, prints a loud backlog banner, and
> grew `--oldest` / `--list`; `daily-onboarding-prompt.md` Step 9 now requires commit + push.
>
> **Remaining backlog: 20 kennels** — 8 config-only (`ph3-cz` · `eels-h3` · `sh2b` · `sierra-h4` HC;
> `dlh3-gb` · `isca-h3` GCal; `jax-h3` iCal source-add; `mickleover-h3` GSheets) and 12 needing new
> bespoke adapters (`tvh3` · `devonport-h3` · `coeh3` · `tamar-h3` · `herts-h3` · `fukfm` · `h5-gb` ·
> `sh4` · `surrey-h3` · `donnington-h3` · `quorn-h3` · `essex-h3`). `2026-06-09-lima-h3` stays a

> **✅ POST-MERGE LIVE (2026-07-16, all prod-verified) — HC BATCH-6.** [PR #2654](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2654)
> merged (`71d5ca1f`); all 6 kennels live: **rio-h3 3 · poznan-h3 3 · divahhh 9 · steel-city-h3 1 ·
> manchester-h3 3 · douliu-h3 31** canonical events (46 backfill + 4 live), **0 cancelled on every
> source** (the `upcomingOnly` reconcile contract held), correct region badges, all 6 `/kennels/*` +
> all 6 `/kennel-logos/*` serve 200. Scoped **Pass 3** created the 4 `scheduleRules` (mh3-gb 3 ·
> douliu-h3 1) via `runKennelSeedPass` + `applyUpserts` (Taoyuan precedent — NOT the global
> `runScheduleRuleBackfill`). Steel City & Manchester shipped **live-only** (0 backfill); the other 4
> are backfill + 0-upcoming (recently-active, expected). Divahhh landed **9** — the same-day #39/#40
> Walkers/Runners collapse guard held in the real merge (both survived, not 8).
>
> 🔴 **Incident during the post-merge seed — importing `prisma/seed.ts` auto-runs the FULL seed.** The
> scoped-seed one-shot `import`ed `seedKennels` from `prisma/seed.ts`, whose **unguarded top-level
> `main().catch()`** fired the entire full seed as an import side effect, running **concurrently** with
> the scoped call against prod. That concurrency (two racing `region.create`s) produced misleading
> `P2002` "unique on (name)" errors on names that didn't exist — not a driver bug, self-inflicted. The
> one-shot's `process.exit(0)` then killed the runaway `main()` **very early, right after region
> creation**, so the blast radius was near-zero: only the 6 batch kennels were touched (no other kennel
> profile overwritten), **zero** other source configs changed (the 17 other sources touched in the hour
> were all routine scrape-cron), and `deactivateStaleRules` never ran (schedule rules 447→451 = only the
> +4 mine, 78 inactive unchanged). **Fix follow-up: [PR #2655](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2655) adds an entry-point guard to
> `prisma/seed.ts`** so importing it is inert (mirrors `scripts/backfill-schedule-rules.ts`). Lesson:
> never `import` from `prisma/seed.ts` in a one-shot; write self-contained upserts, or import from the
> entry-point-guarded `scripts/backfill-schedule-rules.ts` for scoped Pass 3.

> **2026-07-15 — HC BATCH-6 SHIPPED (Rio · Poznan · Divahhh · Steel City · Manchester · Douliu).** The
> six config-only `HARRIER_CENTRAL` handoffs from the 2026-07-10→15 daily runs — **generated but never
> implemented** — were built in **one batch PR** (hc-batch-4 #2622 / hc-batch-10 #2526 precedent). Zero
> new adapter code; **5 new METROs** (Rio de Janeiro / Poznan / Sheffield / Manchester / Douliu; Brussels
> already existed), 6 self-hosted logos, **4 frozen-JSON backfills** (rioh3 3 · ph3-pl 3 · divahhh 9 ·
> douliu-h3 **31** — deeper than the ~25 estimate, back to 2022-07-30), 2 live-only rows. Live-verified
> all 6 `adapter.fetch`: Steel City **1** (#22 2026-07-18), Manchester **3**, and **0-upcoming for Rio /
> Poznan / Divahhh / Douliu** (expected — recently-active). 0 errors, 0 unmatched. tsc + lint + **9,996
> tests** green. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-15-hc-batch-6-retro.md`.
>
> 🔴 **Root cause of the backlog — the daily runs' output was never committed.** All six handoffs (plus
> their run-log entries, 4 platform notes and queue rows 12–15) sat **untracked/uncommitted in the main
> repo**; `origin/main` never moved, so any session branching from `origin/main` saw a "complete"
> handoffs folder and an audit wrongly concluded *"0 kennels left to onboard"* while six live kennels sat
> unimplemented on disk. **This is the same failure the 2026-07-05 catch-up diagnosed** — and whose fix
> ("teach `copy-newest-handoff.sh` to warn when >1 un-implemented handoff exists / add `--oldest`") was
> never built. It recurred at 6× scale. **Fix the pipeline: the daily run must COMMIT its handoff + doc
> edits**, and make the backlog loud.
>
> **Corrections applied during implementation:** (1) 🔴 both GB handoffs said `country: "United Kingdom"`
> — every existing UK kennel + the Cornwall METRO use **`"UK"`** (only `STATE_GROUP_MAP` *values* use
> "United Kingdom") → shipped `"UK"`. (2) 🔴 **Rio shipped 3 region.ts edits, not 4** — the handoff's
> `COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP` metro key is a **dead no-op** (`getCountryGroup` only ever receives
> `getStateGroup`'s output, which already maps Rio→Brazil); the São Paulo row omits it too. *The identical
> mistake hc-batch-4's retro already documented — the research prompt still emits it.* (3) 🔴 **Divahhh
> #39/#40** (Walkers/Runners, same date **and** same 09:30) would have been **silently collapsed** by
> merge (`looksLikeSameEvent` = equal startTime OR runNumber) → cleared the Walkers row's `startTime` so
> both survive; a collapse scanner now reports 0 unresolved pairs across all 4 backfills. (4) Douliu title
> cleaning needed a fragment guard ("DH3 #160 in Douliu" → "in Douliu" is worse than the synth) → 14 real
> themes kept, 17 synthesize. (5) Bandung anchor cited `:7443`/`:7463`, actually **`:7487`** — locate by
> content. (6) 🔴 **Steel City shipped FLAT schedule fields, dropping the handoff's `BYDAY=4SA`** — the
> observed runs fit neither ordinal (2025-05-24 = 4th-not-last; 2025-08-30 = last-not-4th), so a
> HIGH-confidence rule would mis-project every 5-Saturday month and the weekday-based rule-drift cron
> couldn't catch it (Algarve/Lune Valley flat precedent).
>
> **Post-merge = targeted seed + 🔴 SCOPED Pass-3 + 4 backfill runs + 6 scrapes.** 🔴 A targeted
> `seedKennels` does **NOT** create ScheduleRule rows — `runScheduleRuleBackfill` runs only from a full
> `prisma db seed` (`prisma/seed.ts:755`), which we avoid (barbados #2471 config-revert). So mh3-gb (×3)
> and douliu-h3 (×1) need a **scoped** Pass 3 — `runKennelSeedPass(prisma, planned, {}, <2-kennel filter>)`
> + `applyUpserts`, never the global (it touches all 462 rules + `deactivateStaleRules`) — the Taoyuan
> Metro precedent. Without it their rules land nowhere and nothing errors. SHIPPED flips below.
>
> **Also this sweep: `lima-h3` stays BLOCKED (re-verified today).** Local keyed `fetchBloggerPosts`
> (blogId `4935356690439854808`): reachable but frozen at **Hash 765 · 2023-09-07 — 1042 days / 34.2
> months stale, 8 posts**; sibling `limahash.com` dead too (last real post 2024-02-10). Honors the
> handoff's mandatory no-dormant-source gate → not onboarded. Retro added
> (`handoffs/retros/2026-06-09-lima-h3-retro.md`). **No queue edit owed** — `target-queue.md`'s Leads
> section already carries a full dormant record for both Lima hashes (blogId, staleness, recency-gate
> failure, and the post structure for a future revival).
> `2026-06-15-kaohsiung-h3.md` remains **VOID** (self-marked duplicate of the shipped 06-14) — no-op.
> **Retro coverage is now complete for every handoff**: batch retros cover psh3-th/fishhh/beerh3/lvh3-gb
> (`2026-07-05-backlog-catchup-retro.md`), titsh3/lh3-fr/heraultics/krashh3 (`2026-07-09-hc-batch-4-retro.md`),
> h2fmh3 (`2026-07-02-hc-batch-10-retro.md`), and wsh3-ch's build (`2026-05-30-wsh3-retro.md` — the 06-03

Weekly/fortnightly Monday ~7pm. NOT a 0-upcoming case. 🔴 **Forward-widget run numbers UNRELIABLE** (both runs labeled "1931" — site data-entry dup) → adapter identity = kennel+date, runNumber best-effort.
- Historical backfill: **~1,900 runs back to 1990** — published Google Sheet (Run/Date/Day/Venue/Hares, per-year tabs; default tab = 2025 season #1875+) → one-shot `scripts/backfill-mkh3-history.ts` (frozen-JSON + dumb loader, H7 pattern; year-from-tab-title + "DD Mon" → UTC-noon; run-# monotonicity + PII scrub).

> the handoffs said to add metro keys (`"Lyon":"France"`) there, but the group keys France/United
> Kingdom already exist; a metro key is a dead no-op (grouping resolves metro→group via
> `STATE_GROUP_MAP`, then group→country via `COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP`). (2) France inference added
> `montpellier|herault` but **omitted bare `lyon`** (it doubles as US Lyon County KS/NV/IA/MN/KY —
> `france` still catches "Lyon, France"), per the Warsaw/Belgrade discipline and the handoff's own
> caveat. tsc + lint + 9959 tests green. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-09-hc-batch-4-retro.md`.

> **`lima-h3`** (Peru) is the one that stays blocked — re-verified dormant 2026-07-09: the primary blog `limahashash.blogspot.com`
> newest post is **Hash 765 / 2023-09-07 (~1036 days stale, 8 posts total)**, and the sibling
> `limahash.com` (Lima Extra Miércoles, WordPress) last real post is **2024-02-10 (~881 days)**; the
> page's only "2026" string is today's date. No live Lima source → not onboarded (honors the handoff's
> own "don't ship a dormant source" directive). **`tymh3-tw`** (Taoyuan, Taiwan) was blocked across two
> headless attempts — then **SHIPPED 2026-07-09** ([PR #2636](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2636),
> new `BoomCalendarAdapter`). 🔑 **The block was a platform MISID:** the "Calendar" widget is **not** Wix
> Events (that collection is empty — `wix_events_list` → `{"events":[]}`) — it's a third-party **Boom
> Calendar** Wix app (`appDefId 13b4a028-…`, iframe `calendar.boomte.ch`) with its own backend at
> `calendar.apiboomtech.com`. Because the fetch is inside a cross-origin iframe it's invisible to SSR /
> the parent network log, so browserRender (timed out / 429'd) and the Wix Events API (empty collection)
> were both dead ends. Handing the live page to **Claude-in-Chrome** identified Boom Calendar and the
> clean data path: `GET <site>/_api/v1/access-tokens` → `apps["13b4a028-…"].instance`, then
> `GET calendar.apiboomtech.com/api/calendar?comp_id=<compId>&instance=<jwt>` returns all events with
> venue coords in one call. Shipped as a reusable config-driven `BoomCalendarAdapter` (mints a fresh
> instance per scrape) — Taiwan's 6th metro (Taoyuan). Live-verified 3 upcoming (#181–#183, biweekly Fri
> 19:15). Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-09-tymh3-tw-retro.md`. **Lesson: live-verify against a real
> browser before concluding a JS widget is un-scrapeable — and when a Wix calendar widget won't render
> and Wix Events is empty, check the widget `appDefId` in `/_api/v1/access-tokens`; it may be a
> third-party calendar app.**

> implementation: Lune Valley's proposed `abbrev "LAN"` collides with **Lansing, MI** → shipped
> as **`LNC`**, and Lune Valley shipped **flat schedule fields** (skipped the optional `BYSETPOS`
> `scheduleRules`). SHIPPED flips + retro files follow post-merge.

Each run = a `#NNNN Month Day Title` `<h2>` + prose (time/cost/"Meet at …"/`maps.app.goo.gl`) + a `Your Hares:` `<h4>` whose names sit in the following `<h1>`. `config.upcomingOnly:true`, trustLevel 5, scrapeDays 90. Captured the verbatim DOM at build via `curl` (the research sandbox could only read rendered text).
- Dedup: full onboard — **442 slugs** (Chrome MCP 2026-06-14, the audit owed since 2026-06-13), no `kaohsiung*`/`khhh*`/`kh3` slug; only Taiwan-family slugs are `taiwan-h3`+`taipei-h3`. 🔴 kennelCode `kaohsiung-h3` (clear; bare **"KHHH"=Kampong H3**, **"KH3"=Kowloon H3** → both omitted from aliases — the city-based code/shortName discipline, same as `twh3-tw`/`taipei-h3`). est. **16 Sep 1973** (southern Taiwan's oldest).
- Metadata: founded **1973** (`/about`), mixed run types (Sat afternoon ~13:00 / Sat night ~19:00 / Sun family ~09:00 → flat fields + `scheduleNotes`; not over-engineered into `scheduleRules` from an image schedule), walkersWelcome true, FB page `KaohsiungH3` + IG `kaohsiunghash`. **hashCash left blank** (standard cash not published online — the NT$300 was a special joint-run `Event.cost`). Self-hosted Wix logo (`Hashlogopatch.png` → magic-byte-confirmed **PNG** 500×500, NOT trusting the `~mv2.png` suffix).
- Region: **NEW Kaohsiung METRO** under the seeded Taiwan COUNTRY — 2 `region.ts` edits (REGION_SEED_DATA + `STATE_GROUP_MAP`), sky palette like Taipei. 🟢 **COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP entry OMITTED** (the handoff said 3 edits; matched the Taipei precedent — Taipei is absent there and resolves fine, the metro key in that map is dead). `kaohsiung`/`高雄` inference already on `main`.
- Historical backfill: **none** — full 2026 schedule published only as an image (`KHHH V2(2).png`); no machine-readable archive (run #2732 implies deep history but the source exposes none). `upcomingOnly:true` protects the rolling ~2–3-run window from reconcile false-cancel.
- 🔴 Key learnings (→ retro): **(1) Codex adversarial review caught a real PARTIAL-drift hole** — the windowed-empty fail-loud guard only fires when the WHOLE result is empty, so one good run + one date-drifted heading would let `reconcile.ts` false-CANCEL the drifted run's sole-source canonical while the page still lists it. Fix generalizes the single-page guard to **per-numbered-block `ParseError`** (every `#NNNN` heading that won't fully parse → `errors[]` + `errorDetails.parse`). Reusable for any multi-run SSR page. **(2) Self-inflicted review churn** — a Gemini round-2 "use full month names" readability nit pushed `MONTH_RE` over Sonar **S5843** complexity (round 3). The form that satisfied ALL reviewers (and is genuinely best) from the first write would be a **generic word-token matcher (`/\b[a-z]{3,9}\b/gi`) validated by exact `Map.get()`** — no alternation (no S5843/S5852), no slice, no "Maybe"→"May" false positive, no object-injection. **(3) Seed from up-to-date `main`, never the stale worktree** (the `sources.ts` full-overwrite revert hazard, reinforced — 5 PRs had merged after the branch point).

- 🔴 Key learning: when onboarding a kennel whose bare shortcode (`TwH3`→`twh3`) is an existing **kennelCode** for an unrelated kennel (Tidewater), the suffix must go on the kennelCode (`twh3-tw`) AND the bare-shortcode alias must be dropped — kennel resolution does kennelCode-exact-match before alias, so a bare alias silently mis-routes. (Mirrors the Lisbon bare-`LH3` omission, but here the collision is a kennelCode not just an alias.)
- 🔴 Key learning (build-time, CodeRabbit): the handoff called CJK coverage of `COUNTRY_INFERENCE_RULES` "optional" because the English `eventCityAndCountry` ("Taipei, Taiwan") matches and #2662 carries explicit coords — but a Chinese-only location field (`新北市, 台灣`) still defaulted to **"USA"** under the ASCII-`\b` rule. Fixed in-PR by appending a CJK branch `[台臺][灣北中南]|新北|高雄` (the `[台臺]` class unifies the common/formal Tai- forms). For any CJK-locale country, **add the CJK branch, don't mark it optional** — and use a character class for single-char alternations or SonarCloud flags **S6035** (caught here; `(灣|北|中|南)` → `[灣北中南]`).

- Sibling: **Lima Extra Miércoles** (`limahash.com` WordPress, est. 2022, monthly Wed) = SEPARATE kennel — its WP REST works but is dormant (1 hash post Feb-2024) + linked Meetup dead → future source-add, queued in Leads, NOT bundled.
- Historical backfill: archive depth UNKNOWN from sandbox — assess via `fetchBloggerPosts(url,200)` at build; freeze a dataset if deep (Brasília pattern).
- 🔴 Key learning: "reachable" ≠ "active". A Blogspot can be unreachable from the research sandbox (empty feed/HTML + not Google-indexed) yet live for the user — making Claude Code's local live-verify the gating step was right. But the gate must check **recency**, not just reachability: here the keyed Blogger API returned data, yet the newest run was ~33 months old. The handoff's explicit "STOP if newest run is months old" rule did its job — honor it and abort rather than shipping a dormant directory entry. (Both Lima hashes — this blog + the *Extra Miércoles* sibling on `limahash.com` — are dormant; no live Peru source exists yet.)

- 🔴 Key learning (→ retro): the handoff's open coord decision ("accept the Lisbon-centroid pin" vs "drop coords") is really a **TWO-part change** — dropping the pin via `hcGeocodeFailed` is only half; `composeHcLocation` composes the location text independently (its `stripTba` doesn't filter "TBD"/"No location provided"/"ANNOUNCED LATER via Hares"), so coords-only leaves the placeholder string in `event.location` for the merge to store + geocode (Codex P2). Complete fix: a shared `GEOCODE_FAIL_SENTINELS` set drives BOTH `hcGeocodeFailed` (drop coords + `dropCachedCoords`) AND `composeHcLocation` (`stripPlaceholderLocation` → render unlocated). Seeded the set with the whole family (`tbd`/`tbc`/`to be determined`/`to be confirmed`/`to be announced`/`no location provided`/`announced later via hares`) per Gemini/Claude — config-free for future HC kennels. Suppression is EXACT-MATCH so a real coarse location ("Portugal" on #1025) survives. SonarCloud clean (set lookup, not regex). New Harrier Central section added to `source-platform-notes.md`.

Coverage: hares 441/441, coords 418/441, location 435/441.
- 🔴 Key learning (→ retro): the handoff's "trust the in-body `Date:` line, NOT `post.date`" is right for the forward window, but the **11-year hand-typed archive** has 9/442 messy dates (year-less, a `Januray` month typo, copy-pasted stale day+year) that only surface on the full backfill. Fixed with `resolveRunDate`: parse anchored to the **publish date** (recovers year-less + typos), then re-anchor to the named weekday-on/after-publish **only when the parsed date is >45d BEFORE publication** (directional guard from Gemini/claude-review — an absolute `|gap|>45d` would corrupt a legit far-future announcement; measured: 0 far-future, 7 far-past in the archive). Review also fixed an `h`-suffixed-time parse (`7:30pm – 19:30h`→19:30; `9pm – 21:00 but be there for 20:45`→21:00), a `NaN` run-number path (require a leading digit), and reading the Maps URL from the anchor `href` with a **starts-with** selector (a `contains` selector matched a Facebook `l.php?u=…goo.gl/maps…` shim) + trailing-`)` strip. SonarCloud: 2 S5852 hotspots (theme + URL regex, both linear) marked **SAFE** via REST (the `sonarqube` MCP returns 0 PR hotspots); 3 S4325 `as never` → typed `madridSource()` factory. Prod domain is **hashtracks.xyz** (`.com` is parked-for-sale — stale `CLAUDE.md` example). Post-merge from MAIN repo (worktree has no `.env`).
- 🩹 **Post-ship cleanup 2026-06-09** → [PR #2050](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2050) (merged), CHROME_KENNEL audit #2040/#2041/#2039. **#2040 title/description SWAP (442/442):** the ship-time "synthesize title + theme-in-description" decision was a bug — `The "X" R*n` *is* the real per-event title. Adapter now routes the post title to `title`, `description: null`; backfill re-run **created=4 / updated=442**, prod now 0 placeholder titles / 0 stale descriptions (retro item #3 annotated REVERSED). **#2041 gap:** full WP-REST enumeration = 453 posts → 442 parseable runs (the "~10 missing" are posts with no `Run No.` label, not truly absent); curated **+4** real runs with explicit in-body dates (3× Jun-2025 + Xmas-2020), documented 6 dateless/COVID posts as excluded → **446 events**. Regenerated frozen JSON via new `scripts/generate-madrid-h3-history.ts` (full enumeration + shared `extractMadridPostBody`). **#2039:** foundedYear 1984 + self-hosted logo already correct (kept local over the audit's remote-URL suggestion). No orphan canonicals (date stable, sourceUrl-matched in place; the one same-day pair is a legit double-header #2335/#2336).

Plus the **österreich `\b` non-ASCII regex** dead-token (Gemini). Sonar S7755/S7735 fixed at source (no NOSONAR). **Worktree path gotcha AGAIN** (5th+ — region.ts edits landed in MAIN; recovered via `diff`→`checkout`→`git apply` into the worktree). Declined claude-review's "scheduleTime 24h" nit (12-hour is the convention for the legacy flat field).

## 2026-06-05 — KL Junior H3 (Kuala Lumpur, MY) — done (already live)
- Target attempted: Rank 13 KL Junior H3. **Already shipped — queue row was stale.**
- Outcome: **done (already live)** — kennel (`kennels.ts:3651`), source (`sources.ts:4350`), and `KljH3Adapter` (`klj-h3.ts`, registry.ts:237) all present; `/kennels/klj-h3` live with **7 upcoming + 93 past** events (Run #526, next run 2026-06-07). - Follow-ups: **dual-source trust ordering** — website bumped to trust 8 > sheet 7 so its coords aren't dropped by the merge's lower-trust enrichment path (Codex catch; merge regression test added). Spawned task: make that enrichment path backfill coords/`locationAddress` symmetrically with `locationName`. Retro at `handoffs/retros/2026-06-02-nswhhh-retro.md`: enumerate ALL sheet gids for history; trust ordering follows coord ownership; `csvUrl` bypasses the `GOOGLE_CALENDAR_API_KEY` gate; `sitesv` logos 403 server-side; query the real Sonar hotspot via REST (bots guessed the wrong regex line).
